# 🐭 Abominable Snow Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

> "Abominable" redirects here. For other uses, see Abominable (disambiguation).

| Abominable Snow Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Mountain Mice |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 5,000 | Gold: | 5,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro or Law | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Abominable Asiago Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Mountain | Loot: | Divine OrbSuper Power CharmAncient Relic |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Abominable Snow mice are far from bashful. They love to play innocent pranks that always seem to involve pelting others with a barrage of snowballs. These mice are occasionally seen wandering down from the Mountains to give hunters a hard time. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Abominable Snow Mouse is attracted only to Abominable Asiago Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Abominable Snow Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | AllexceptDraconic, Parental and Rift | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Abominable Snow Mouse only in the Mountain location.

## Loot

The Abominable Snow Mouse drops the following loot in the Mountain location:

- Divine Orb
- Super Power Charm
- Ancient Relic

## History and Trivia

- The name and visual appearance of the Abominable Snow Mouse are based on common incarnations of the Abominable Snowman legend.
- 11 December 2008: The Abominable Snow Mouse was released.
- Christmas 2008: The Mountain became the sole habitat of the Abominable Snow Mouse.
- 16 November 2009: The Abominable Snow Mouse was chosen as the first "Monday Mystery Mouse".
- 25 January 2011: The Abominable Snow Mouse began to drop Divine Orbs as loot.
- 25 June 2013: The Abominable Snow Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Abominable Snow Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 7 November 2017: The Abominable Snow Mouse image was updated and began to drop Super Power Charms as loot. Gold and points were increased from 1,750 each, to 5,000 each.
- 17 October 2018: The Abominable Snow Mouse changed mouse groups from Indigenous Mice to Mountain Mice.

# 🐭 Absolute Acolyte Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Absolute Acolyte Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Bristle Woods Rift. It is the Rift counterpart of Acolyte Mouse

| Absolute Acolyte Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Rift Stalkers (Bristle Woods Rift) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 1,000,000 | Gold: | 300,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Rift | Other Requirements: |  |
| Cheese: | Runic String Cheese | Charm: |  |
| Locations: | Bristle Woods Rift | Loot: | Mysterious Rift BoxMysterious Bronze Rift BoxMysterious Silver Rift BoxMysterious Gold Rift BoxAncient RelicBelow when Frozen Alcove is activeTiny SprocketClockwork CogTimesplit RuneTimesplit CharmRift Antiskele CharmRift Vacuum CharmRift Super Vacuum CharmRift Super Luck CharmRift Extreme Luck CharmRift Ultimate Luck CharmRift Ultimate Lucky Power CharmRift Ultimate Power CharmUltimate CharmMagic EssenceMagical String CheeseRift Rumble CheeseRunic String Cheese PotionAncient String Cheese PotionQuantum QuartzPortal ScramblerCalcified Rift MistBer EssenceRift CurdBristle Woods Rift Gift BasketBristle Woods Rift Journal Theme Scrap III |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Phenomenal cosmic power bound only by the Bristle Woods Rift, these terrifying mice of immense influence and control over time are a mere whisker away from breaking free from their confinements. Entwined in endless strings of time, altered by previous attempts at escape, it is unknown if the Absolute Acolyte Mouse will be able to eventually break free or perhaps already has, but we must do everything in our power to prevent this from occurring in any timeline! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 865 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Absolute Acolyte Mouse has been found to be especially attracted to Runic String Cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Rift | None | None |

## Hunting Strategy

Only Rift type traps are very effective against Absolute Acolyte Mouse, all other power types are ineffective.

Absolute Acolyte Mouse can only be attracted upon fully charging the Timesplit Obelisk in the Acolyte Chamber and can only be caught after fully draining the Acolyte’s sand.

Completing the Frozen Alcove will prevent the Absolute Acolyte from pillaging the Hunter's trap and it will add additional loot to the Absolute Acolyte when caught.

## History and Trivia

- 31 May 2017: The Absolute Acolyte Mouse was introduced as part of the Bristle Woods Rift release.

# 🐭 Acolyte Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

> "Acolyte" redirects here. For other uses, see Acolyte (disambiguation).

| Acolyte Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: The Forgotten Mice |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 31,850 | Gold: | 36,309 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Forgotten | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Runic Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Acolyte Realm | Loot: | Ancient RelicMysterious Box |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Years ago, the Acolyte mice brought necromancy to the Kingdom. Their undead creations rose from the depths of the Catacombs, with the sole purpose of destroying Gnawnia. Hunters’ lore states that the first-ever MouseHunter devised a way to turn the spells of the Forgotten Book against the Acolytes and banish them to another realm of existence, somewhere between the dead and the living. Had it not been for Plankrun’s heroic efforts, the small village of Gnawnia may never have become the prosperous town it is today. Now that they have returned, the Acolyte mice are amassing their creations, preparing to achieve their dark destiny: the eradication of each and every MouseHunter! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 2 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Acolyte Mouse is attracted only to Runic Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Acolyte Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Forgotten | None | ShadowArcane |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Acolyte Mouse only in the Acolyte Realm location.

## Loot

The Acolyte Mouse drops the following loot in the Acolyte Realm location:

- Mysterious Box
- Ancient Relic

## History and Trivia

- The Acolyte Mouse was first discovered after a glitch occurred while the developers were clearing out the account of a known scammer.

- On the Dojo header image, the two mice standing guard are Acolyte Mice.

- Acolyte Mice caught before 25 March 2009 did not drop Mysterious Boxes.

- On 30 September 2009, the Acolyte Mouse began to drop Frozen Scrolls alongside Mysterious Boxes. Hunters with previously unopened Mysterious Boxes were credited a Frozen Scroll, while those with a Frozen Scroll were given another Mysterious Box.

- In MouseHunt 3.0, the Acolyte Mouse no longer drops a Frozen Scroll. Instead the Mysterious Box now always gives a Frozen Scroll plus one random item.

- The Acolyte Mouse began dropping Ancient Relics as loot on 29 August 2012.

# 🐭 Admiral Cloudbeard

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

| Admiral Cloudbeard |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Floating Islanders (Sky Pirate) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 388,500 | Gold: | 60,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | See Power Type below | Other Requirements: | See Island/Vault Info below |
| Cheese: | Sky Pirate Swiss Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Floating Islands | Loot: | See Loot below |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Behold the "Sultan of the Skies", the "Conqueror of the Clouds", the "Tyrant at Ten Thousand Feet": Admiral Cloudbeard, leader of the Sky Pirates! This malevolent marauder is undefeated in hand-to-hook combat thanks to his massive, untamed beard making him an impossible target. His opponents can never tell where the beard ends and the Admiral begins. It's so thick that swords get stuck in it and shots get completely absorbed. Legend has it that his mighty beard has even survived being shot with a cannonball!<br>There is only one sky dweller brave enough to face the Admiral head-on and capable enough to come out of it alive and intact, his arch-nemesis: Ronza! The Admiral and his legion of ne'er-do-wells have been hunting Ronza and her enormous cache of chrome treasures for as long as they've sailed the skies. Unfortunately for them, she has consistently managed to elude, confuse, defeat, and humiliate Cloudbeard and his cronies at every turn. The Admiral has vowed to never give up on his quest for vengeance against that legendary sky captain, no matter how many limbs it costs him.<br>♫ We pirate, pillage, and plight,<br>Attacking every ship in flight!<br>Those that oppose, face the beard that he grows,<br>And he'll never give up the fight! ♫<br>- An ancient sky shanty |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1014 |  |  |

## Power Type

- Physical - Very Effective
- Shadow - Very Effective
- Tactical - Very Effective
- Arcane - Very Effective
- Forgotten - Very Effective
- Hydro - Very Effective
- Draconic - Very Effective
- Law - Very Effective

## Island/Vault Info

- To encounter the Admiral Cloudbeard, the Hunter must pick an island or vault with at least 2 Sky Pirate Dens on the Sky Map or Launch Console respectively.

## Loot

### Floating Islands's Sky Pirate Loot

Admiral Cloudbeard has a chance to drop Storm Cell if Rocket Boosters have been attached on the Hunter's Dirigible and the following loot depending on the buffs present:

| Item | Condition |
| --- | --- |
| Sky Glass | Always drops Boosted by Sky Glass Formations and Loot Caches |
| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |
| Sky Pirate Seal | Always Drops The amount a mouse drops depends on the mouse caught. |
| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

- Additionally, the Admiral Cloudbeard can drop the following loot as well:
Bottled Wind
Chrome Charm
Cyclone Stone
Rift Tarnished Charm
Storm Cell
Tarnished Charm
Wealth Charm

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Admiral Cloudbeard was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

## Cheese Preference

Adventure Weaver is attracted to Plotting Paneer or Storied Saga or Deus Ex Maconnais.

## Hunting Strategy

The Adventure Weaver can only be attracted while writing a postscript to a story.

Only Law power types are effective against Adventure Weaver.
All other types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 13 January 2026: Adventure Weaver was released as part of Conclusion Cliffs location.

# 🐭 Architeuthulhu of the Abyss

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

Architeuthulhu of the Abyss is a breed of mouse found in Prologue Pond.

| Architeuthulhu of the Abyss |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Prologue Pond Prowlers (Deep Divers) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 100,000 | Gold: | 30,000 |

| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| In the darkest depths of the Prologue Pond, a great and terrible beast waits dreaming. It makes its home amongst the sunken wrecks of foolish adventurers who sought its treasure. Some believe it is as old as the pond itself while others claim it is far older still. A being born from the darkest corners of the subconscious, meant only to reside in legends too terrifying to speak aloud. A being that could only be brought into existence by the awesome and transcendent power of the Folklore Forest.<br>Whatever its mystical origins may be, the threat of the mighty Architeuthulhu is very real and very present in the deepest waters of the Prologue Pond. Fortunately, timid hunters can avoid the piercing gaze of its haunting yellow eyes by simply not wandering too far out from shore and staying away from inclement weather. However, hunters who are brave enough to take on this indomitable challenge must summon a storm as vile and menacing as Architeuthulu's wrath by arming Stormy Clamembert cheese. Once this volatile cheese breaks the surface of the water, Architeuthulhu will rise from its abyss with all of its valuable treasures in tow! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1091 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

Architeuthulhu of the Abyss is only attracted to Stormy Clamembert Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Hydro power type is very effective against Architeuthulhu of the Abyss.
All other types are ineffective.

## Loot

Architeuthulhu of the Abyss can drop:

- Ancient Relic
- Condensed Creativity
- Flawless Orb

Additionally, it can drop the following loot depending on Chum status:

- Pond Penny (when Brainstorm Chum off)
- Inspiration Ink (when Brainstorm Chum on)

## History and Trivia

- 26 July 2022: Architeuthulhu of the Abyss was released as part of Prologue Pond location.

# 🐭 Artillery Commander Mouse

The Artillery Commander Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Fiery Warpath. They are known to drop the Artillery Strike Launch Box as loot.

| Artillery Commander Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: The Marching Flame (Command) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 250,000 | Gold: | 500,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Arcane | Other Requirements: | See Hunting Strategy |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Fiery Warpath | Loot: | Artillery Strike Launch Box |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Tucked away inside the Warmonger's top secret Command Tent, this stout mouse holds on to the Artillery Strike Launch Box which he uses to command an elite strike force of bombers to wipe out targets from a safe distance. Evidence of this weapon can be seen from the burning rubble that remains at Muridae. He sits waiting patiently for the orders to strike! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 885 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Artillery Commander Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

The Artillery Commander Mouse can be encountered and attracted in the Artillery Commander's Tent, but is immune to capture until five Theurgy Warden Mice have been caught. Using an Ultimate Charm will override this immunity.

Arcane weapons are the most effective for capturing the Artillery Commander Mouse but Physical, Tactical or Hydro weapons can also be used to catch it.

The Artillery Commander Mouse mice can only be encountered by entering the Artillery Commander's Tent using a Warpath Portal Projector during Wave 4 in the Fiery Warpath.

Upon catching the Artillery Commander Mouse, the following message is displayed in the Hunter's Journal:
As I captured Artillery Commander Mouse, a strange metallic suitcase clunked onto the ground. Flipping it open, I was faced with an odd electronic interface which is used to call down an artillery strike against waves of mice in the Fiery Warpath!

## History and Trivia

- 15 August 2017: The Artillery Commander Mouse is released with the expansion of the Fiery Warpath area.

Focused on finding "oneness" through menial but meaningful work, their training has come full circle, back to where students begin. Sweeping the grand floors of the Grand Pagoda, they find greater meaning, purpose, humility and spiritual wholeness through this act of cleanliness and self-sacrifice.<br>They may look hapless and powerless, but don't let that fool you... Striking one down will make them become more powerful than you can possibly imagine. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 819 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Ascended Elder Mouse has been found to be especially attracted to Ascended Cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Hunting Strategy

Only Rift type traps are very effective against Ascended Elder Mouse, all other power types are ineffective.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Ascended Elder Mouse drops the Ascended Elder Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

### Other Events

- 2016

> Royal Challenge

## History and Trivia

- 18 May 2016: Ascended Elder Mouse was released with the release of Furoma Rift.

### Cheese

The Assassin Mouse is attracted mainly to Maki Cheese and SUPER|brie+.

No Charms are required to attract the Assassin Mouse.

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tactical | Physical | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Assassin Mouse only in the Dojo location. It is very rarely encountered.

## Loot

The Assassin Mouse drops the following loot in the Dojo location:

- Ambush Trap Blueprints
- Ancient Relic
- Satchel of Gold (1,000 gold)

The Ambush Trap Blueprints are required to construct the Ambush weapon.

## History and Trivia

- 20 November 2008: The Assassin Mouse was released in the Training Grounds..
- 27 November 2008: The Assassin Mouse's habitat expanded to the Dojo when Hunters accessed the Furoma Pagoda locations for the first time.
- 2 December 2008: The Assassin Mouse began to drop the Ambush Trap Blueprints as loot.
- 11 December 2008: The Assassin Mouse began to drop a Satchel of Gold worth 1,000 gold as loot.
- 8 June 2010: The Assassin Mouse was removed from the Training Grounds location during the major update to MouseHunt v3.0 (Longtail).
- 4 April 2012: The Assassin Mouse began dropping a bonus Assassin Egg as loot during the Spring Egg Hunt 2012 event.
- 29 August 2012: The Assassin Mouse began dropping Ancient Relics as loot.
- 29 July 2015: The Assassin Mouse has an increased chance of dropping Ambush Trap Blueprints.

## History and Trivia

- Balack the Banished was first introduced as Balack the Betrayed after a news ticker message appeared on 7 May 2010 reading "Balack the Betrayed is coming..." Additional teaser messages were provided for several days until a banner appeared on the MouseHunt page, declaring Balack would return 17 May 2010.
- Balack the Banished was originally named Balack the Banished Mouse. Its name was changed with the release of MouseHunt Version 3.0.
- 23 August 2011: Balack the Banished began to drop Lich Jewel as loot.
- 29 August 2012: Balack the Banished began to drop Ancient Relic as loot.
- 30 March 2021: Balack the Banished began to drop Balack the Banished Egg as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2021 event.
- 17 January 2023: Balack the Banished began to drop Pepper Seeds, Bottled-Up Rage, Delicious Stones, Empowered Anchor Charms, Flawless Orbs, Pinch of Annoyance, Raisins of Wrath, Savoury Vegetables, Seashells, and Vanilla Beans as loot.

## Preferred Cheese

The Barmy Gunner Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Hunters must have an active Pirate Bounty to attract this mouse.

Hunters must capture a Pirate Mouse to obtain a pirate bounty. When Hunters begin the search for the mice listed on the crew manifest provided by the Pirate Mouse, the 5 Pirate Crew mice are added to the mouse population of the Harbour. Capturing all 3 mice listed on the crew manifest will reward the player with booty from the pirate bounty.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Barmy Gunner Mouse drops the Pirate Crew Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- 11 April 2017: The Barmy Gunner Mouse was added to the game with the introduction of new content to the Harbour location.

# 🐭 Battering Ram Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Battering Ram is a breed of mouse found in Fort Rox. They are not known to drop any loot.

| Battering Ram Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Meteorite Miners (Special) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 900 | Gold: | 100 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | ArcaneShadow | Other Requirements: | Any Night Phase |
| Cheese: | Standard Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Fort Rox | Loot: | None |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Well-dressed and with a wide grin, this cowboy has a gleam of meteorite shining in his eyes and he won't stop until he gets more! Wielding a solid column of meteorite rock, you better catch him before he catches your walls!<br>When attacking your wall with his battering ram, it produces so much force that it explodes into a cloud of miniscule fragments and dust.<br>He will cause damage to your fort wall even if caught, so avoid him at all costs!<br>Rumor has it that this mouse was specially trained in the light of the moon, which gives him his frenzied strength. However, he ironically hates the taste and sight of Crescent and Moon Cheese, recoiling from the imitation moonlight and running off to protect his precious columns of rock! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 830 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Battering Ram Mouse prefers Standard Cheeses, and will avoid traps baited with Crescent or Moon Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Arcane and Shadow traps are less effective against the Battering Ram Mouse. Other Power Types are ineffective.
The Battering Ram Mouse can be encountered during the Twilight, Midnight, Pitch, Utter Darkness, and First Light phases at Nighttime and at Dawn while hunting with Standard Cheeses in Fort Rox. The Battering Ram is extremely difficult to catch; one strategy is to wait until the Power Enchantment upgrade has been completed, then hunt with Tower Mana activated so as to have a chance at an instant defeat. The Moonbeam Barrier Trap has a 100% catch rate for this mouse.

## History and Trivia

- 3 November 2016: The Battering Ram Mouse was release as a part of Fort Rox update.

## History and Trivia

- In the first month that the Big Bad Burroughs Mouse was released, MouseHunt developers hosted a contest to determine the top three hunters who could catch the most of this breed. Prizes were awarded in the amounts of $50 of SUPER|brie+ (3,500 pieces), $25 of SUPER|brie+ (1,600 pieces), and $10 of SUPER|brie+ (500 pieces), respectively.
- 24 March 2010: The Big Bad Burroughs Mouse is released for the first time.
- 14 April 2011: The Big Bad Burroughs Mouse began dropping a bonus Big Bad Burroughs Egg as loot during the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 29 August 2012: The Big Bad Burroughs Mouse began dropping Ancient Relics as loot.
- 9 September 2014: The Big Bad Behemoth Burroughs Mouse was released as the Rift version of the Big Bad Burroughs Mouse.

# 🐭 Black Powder Thief Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Black Powder Thief is a breed of mouse found in the Gnawnian Express Station. It can only be attracted and caught during the Daredevil Canyon phase and only with Black Powder Charms armed. They are known to drop Fuel Nuggets, Magmatic Crystal Charms, and Tin Scraps as loot.

| Black Powder Thief Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Train Robbers (Fueler) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 9,000 | Gold: | 8,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Law | Other Requirements: | Daredevil Canyon Stage |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | Black Powder Charm |
| Locations: | Gnawnian Express Station | Loot: | Ancient RelicFuel NuggetMagmatic Crystal CharmTin Scrap |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Hot with black powder fever, these mice feel the need, no, the unnatural compulsion to seek out and hoard as much black powder as possible. The sight of Black Powder Charms causes these mice to go into a frenzy! Rolling in it, caressing it, smelling it and some even eating it.They carry Magmatic Crystal Charms and Fuel Nuggets which they drop when caught, but they are no substitute for the real thing and they will stop at nothing to obtain as much black powder as possible!Use Black Powder Charms to attract this mouse who may drop one or more Magmatic Crystal Charms when caught. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 489 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Black Powder Thief mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese, but can only be attracted when a hunter has a Black Powder Charm armed.

## Hunting Strategy

Law type traps such as S.L.A.C. are very effective against the Black Powder Thief Mouse.

## Event Participation

- The Black Powder Thief Mouse drops the Gnawnian Express Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Released on 26 September 2013 as part of the Gnawnian Express Station content.

## Power Type Effectiveness

Because the Black Widow Mouse is rarely encountered, Hunters should arm weapons that are effective against the majority of the mice they will encounter in their current location.

| Very Effective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | All Power Types except Parental | None |

## History and Trivia

- The Black Widow Mouse was the first visually female breed to be released.
- 5 August 2009 through 10 August 2010: The Black Widow Mouse was counted as one of the Rare Breeds (along with a variety of Prize Mice and Special Event Mice) that could help hunters earn a Bonus Mice Badge. The Black Widow Mouse was subsequently removed from the Rare Breeds list and now counts towards the Breeds Caught total as a member of the Indigenous group.
- 14 April 2011: The Black Widow Mouse began dropping a bonus Black Widow Egg as loot during the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 29 August 2012: The Black Widow Mouse began dropping the Ancient Relic as loot.
- 25 June 2013: The Black Widow Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 19 September 2013: As part of the Gnawnia mice population shift, the Black Widow Mouse was removed from the Meadow and Town of Gnawnia locations.
- 25 March 2014: The Black Widow Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.

## Preferred Cheese

Blacksmith mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese, but can only be attracted when a hunter has an Artisan Charm armed.

## History and Trivia

- Blacksmith Mice were introduced on 2 June 2011 with the release of the Muridae Market location.
- Along with the Mage Weaver mouse, it was the first mouse to be introduced that required a specific charm to be armed in order to attract it.
- On 25 June 2013, they gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.

# 🐭 Blight Incarnate

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Blight Incarnate is a breed of mouse found in Afterword Acres.

| Blight Incarnate |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Afterword Anti-Arborists (Blight Bringers) |  |  |  |

| This massive mound of malevolent material is a sentient disease with the sole purpose of ultimate domination over the Afterword Acres. It consumes and infects anything and everything in its path which results in horrifically mutated plant and animal life that, once infected, exist only to serve the abhorrent agenda of this vile virus.<br>This abominable blight was created by the Mythweaver using the limitless power of Idea Germs as a means of conquering the great Infinitree in the Afterword Acres. His goal is to stop the Infinitree from supplying the Folklore Forest with its ability to create powerful new thoughts and infinitely renewable positivity.<br>Beware this horrid creature for, unlike its infected underlings, it spreads its wretched blight by massive amounts and it leaves its mark on the land regardless of whether or not it has been caught. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1224 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

Blight Incarnate is only attracted to Symbolic Sirene.

## Hunting Strategy

Tactical power type is effective against Blight Incarnate.
All other types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 2 September 2025: The Blight Incarnate was introduced with the release of the Afterword Acres location.

| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |

| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |

| Cheese: | Marble StringSwiss StringBrie StringMagical String | Charm: | None |

| The ancient rift ancestor of the Sylvan Mouse in full bloom! They are quite scarce and skittish making them difficult to spot amongst the forest and foliage. Once the flower on the tip of their tail opens and their facial blossoms bloom this mouse becomes quite a rare sight to behold! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 707 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Bloomed Sylvan mice have been found to be especially attracted to String Cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Hunting Strategy

The Bloomed Sylvan Mouse is weak to Rift Traps. It can only be attracted by String Cheese.

The Bloomed Sylvan Mouse is only available when rage in the Crazed Clearing section in Whisker Woods Rift is between 0-24.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Bloomed Sylvan Mouse drops the Enlarged Rift Egg and the Rift Mist Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Bloomed Sylvan mice were introduced on 28 January 2015, with the introduction of the Whisker Woods Rift.
- Bloomed Sylvan mice are the Rift version of Sylvan mice.

| Highly irradiated, the Bog Beast Mouse has developed a penchant to smother himself in the nearby polluted, slimy bog. The muddy slime soothes the Bog Beast's skin, bringing his body and core temperatures back to a more normal level. Be careful wandering through the polluted bog at the Toxic Spill, that pile of mud might not be what you think! |  |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Bog Beast mice are attracted to Magical Rancid Radioactive Blue at every spill level and to Rancid Radioactive Blue when spill level is for Hero to Duke/Duchess.

## Hunting Strategy

Hydro type traps such as Oasis Water Node Trap or Phantasmic Oasis Trap are effective against the Bog Beast Mouse.

## History and Trivia

- Released on 21 January 2014 as part of the Toxic Spill content.
- Bog Beast' mice began dropping Scum Scrubber Blueprints as loot on 12 February 2014 as part of the release of the second spill.

- 9 December 2014: Borean Commander was released as part of the Great Winter Hunt 2014 event, but could not be attracted yet.
- 23 December 2014: Borean Commander became available as part of the third week of the Great Winter Hunt 2014.
- Catching the Borean Commander during the Great Winter Hunt 2014 event created the following entry in the Hunter's Journal:

> I caught the Borean Commander and caused her to flee to safety! Without leaders, the mice of the Crystal Citadel also flee to the fields, and what passes for peace returns again to Gnawnia. I now have until all this ice melts to try to catch the Stuck Snowball Mouse as many times as I can for prizes and loot!

- 13 December 2016: The points/gold for Borean Commander were changed.

Boulder Biter Mouse can only be attracted when the Mist Meter is in Green Zone.

Rift power type is effective against Boulder Biter Mouse while all others are less effective.

## History and Trivia

- 9 September 2014: The Boulder Biter Mouse was released as part of the Burroughs Rift location.

# 🐭 Bounty Hunter Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

| Bounty Hunter Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Wild Bunch (Misc) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 6,000 | Gold: | 4,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Law | Other Requirements: |  |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: |  |
| Locations: | Claw Shot City | Loot: | Sealed Wanted PosterSheriff's Badge Charm |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| This lonesome vigilante creates quite the scene when he enters town. His appearance often signals trouble and a whole heap of it. Shopkeepers often bar their doors and windows while he wanders the streets, as his reckless style tends to leave smoking ruins wherever he passes. Extremely dangerous and self-sufficient, it is said that this mouse is known to honour just one authority figure: the Sheriff.<br>Capturing this mouse will earn you the Sealed Wanted Poster he's carrying. Track down the gang and earn a bounty for it! The Bounty Hunter Mouse won't stick around if you're already chasing down a bounty.<br>This mouse will bend to the authority of the local Sheriff. Hunters with a Sheriff's Badge Charm equipped will instantly catch the Bounty Hunter Mouse and collect two Sealed Wanted Posters from him. Easier than licking dust off of a barn! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 467 |  |  |

## Hunting Strategy

Law type traps such as S.L.A.C. are very effective against the Bounty Hunter Mouse.
The Bounty Hunter Mouse can only be encountered if the Hunter is not doing a Wanted Poster.
The Bounty Hunter Mouse is guaranteed to drop a Sealed Wanted Poster upon capture.
Hunters with a Sheriff's Badge Charm equipped are guaranteed to catch the next Bounty Hunter Mouse they encounter and loot 2 Sealed Wanted Posters, even without using a Law trap. The mouse attraction rate remains the same.

If the Hunter already has the maximum limit of 6 Sealed Wanted Poster, the Bounty Hunter Mouse will not drop one, with the following message appearing in the Hunter's Journal:

> The Bounty Hunter Mouse didn't leave me a Sealed Wanted Poster as I have the maximum allowed by the Marshall of Varmint Valley.

## History and Trivia

- 25 June 2013: The Bounty Hunter Mouse was released as part of the Claw Shot City content.
- 14 June 2022: Sealed Wanted Posters are now a guaranteed loot drop and the Bounty Hunter Mouse can now be encountered even when Hunters have Sealed Wanted Posters in their inventory. The message that used to be displayed in the Hunter's Journal upon capturing the Bounty Hunter Mouse was removed:

> I've caught a Bounty Hunter! He was carrying a Sealed Wanted Poster, which are special bounties issued from the Marshall of Varmint Valley! I should unfurl the Wanted Poster, bring a friend along as my Deputy, and start hunting down one of the Wild Bunch mouse gangs!
While I'm actively hunting down a Wild Bunch gang, the Bounty Hunter Mouse will avoid my trap until he can find a new poster for me.

| Born to Rift! This mouse spends all day Rifting, sets upon sets of Rifts. If it wasn't obvious already, all he does is Rift! His shiny, shimmering, splendid muscles gleam bright white in the sun, showing off all of his hard work. He is, however, not as bright as his brawn. He is more likely to get caught in your trap trying to bench it instead of going after the cheese.Looking at the other mice he thinks, "Do they even Rift?" |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 568 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

In Gnawnia Rift, Brawny mice have been found to be especially attracted to String cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

In Furoma Rift, the Brawny Mouse is only attracted to Marble String or Swiss String Cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Brawny Mouse drops the Enlarged Rift Egg in the Gnawnia Rift during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

### Other Events

- 2017

> MouseHunt's Ninth Birthday

- 2016

> MouseHunt's Eighth Birthday

- 2015

> Great Winter Hunt 2015
MouseHunt's Seventh Birthday

- 2014

> Halloween 2014

## History and Trivia

- Brawny mice are the Rift version of White mice.
- 25 March 2014: Brawny mice were introduced with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 18 May 2016: The Brawny Mouse was released in Furoma Rift.

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Brown Mouse prefers Standard Cheese, but will avoid traps baited with White Cheddar Cheese.

In the Mountain location, the Brown Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with White Cheddar Cheese, Chedd-Ore Cheese, or Abominable Asiago.
In the Windmill location, the Brown Mouse will not be encountered when hunting with White Cheddar Cheese or Grilled Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Brown Mouse.

## Power Type Effectiveness

| Very Effective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | All except Parental | None |

## Loot

The Brown Mouse drops various loot in the following locations:

- Meadow and Town of Gnawnia

> Marble Cheese

- Windmill

> Packet of Flour

The Brown Mouse is not known to drop any loot in the Harbour, King's Arms, or Mountain locations.

## History and Trivia

- 7 March 2008: The Brown Mouse was introduced with the release of MouseHunt.
- 25 June 2013: The Brown Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Riftweaver Mouse was introduced as the Rift counterpart of the Brown Mouse.
- 25 March 2014: The Brown Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 2 September 2014: The Brown Mouse began dropping Packet of Flour at the Windmill.

| Points: | 80,000 | Gold: | 12,000 |

| Required Power Types: | Rift - Very Effective | Other Requirements: | See Floor Type Info below |
| Cheese: | Prefers Gauntlet String CheeseStandard String Cheeses | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Valour Rift | Loot: | See Loot below |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| An unstoppable force poured into an immovable set of armour. This mouse acts as a wall to stop adventures from progressing any further up the infinite floors of the Rift Gauntlet Tower. With one mighty blow of his shield, this mouse will send you tumbling 10 steps back down the tower.<br>When you come to this mouse and you can't go through it and you can't knock it down, you know what you found...<br>The Bulwark! (WHAT?) The Bulwark! The Bulwark! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1000 |  |  |

## Floor Type Info

- The Bulwark of Ascent Mouse can only be encountered after entering the Valour Rift Tower with the Ultimate Umbra augmentation active.
Failing to catch the Bulwark of Ascent Mouse will reduce the Hunter's steps by 10, though the Hunter cannot be pushed beyond their current floor.
- The Bulwark of Ascent Mouse can be found on All Floors except Eclipse.
- The Bulwark of Ascent Mouse has a Minimum Floor of 1.

## Loot

- Divine Orb
- Simple Orb
- The Gilded Steps - Can only be obtained at Floor 100+ of the Valour Rift tower.

## History and Trivia

- 08 October 2019: The Bulwark of Ascent Mouse was introduced with the release of the Valour Rift.
- The "runes" on the Bulwark of Ascent Mouse's shield are the same kind found on the back page of Plankrun's Whisker Woods Rift Notes, and can be deciphered to read "YOU SHALL NOT PASS" when read bottom to top and then right to left, with both sides of the shield having the same text. Likewise, the "runes" on its shoulder read "FALL".

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| Points: | 1,250 | Gold: | 1,250 |

| Required Power Types: | Any type | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Varies by Location(see chart below) | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | BazaarHarbourKing's ArmsLaboratoryTournament Hall | Loot: | Brie CheeseFlawless OrbGilded CheeseGreater Radioactive Blue PotionPlankrun's Journal Back Cover |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| These mice have earned the nickname 'Sparky' after a certain infamous MouseHunter who had very long fingers. With an insatiable lust for brie, these stealthy mice make their way from region to region stealing brie from shoppe keepers! Since they are known for their desire to hoard cheese, a hunter is sure to find a few pieces of left over brie in the satchel of this sparky little mouse. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 26 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese and Loot by Location

| Region | Location | PreferredCheese | Loot |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gnawnia | Harbour | Brie CheeseSUPER&#124;brie+ | Brie CheeseFlawless OrbGilded Cheese |
| Valour | King's Arms | Brie CheeseGilded CheeseSUPER&#124;brie+ | Brie CheeseGilded Cheese |
| Tournament Hall | Brie CheeseSUPER&#124;brie+ | Brie CheeseGilded Cheese |  |
| Burroughs | Laboratory | Brie CheeseSUPER&#124;brie+ | Brie CheeseFlawless OrbGilded CheeseGreater Radioactive Blue Potion |
| Bazaar | Brie CheeseGilded CheeseGouda CheeseSUPER&#124;brie+ | Brie CheeseFlawless OrbGilded CheesePlankrun's Journal Back Cover |  |

## Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Burglar Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

The Burglar Mouse has a predominate weakness to Law weapons, but is found in locations were using a Law trap would be less effective against the other mice that Hunters will encounter.
It is recommended that Hunters use a trap that will be effective against the main mouse population in their hunting location.

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Law | PhysicalShadowTacticalArcaneForgottenHydroDraconicRift | None |

## History and Trivia

- The nickname for the Burglar Mouse did indeed come from a certain notorious scammer. He was believed to be the first to try to repeatedly get honest players to part with their SUPER|brie+ for free on the forums.
- The Burglar Mouse was originally also available in the Town of Gnawnia, as well as several other locations.
- 23 May 2008: The Burglar Mouse is released
- 8 June 2010: The Burglar Mouse began to drop Gilded Cheese as loot with the release of MouseHunt version 3.0.
- 25 January 2011: The Burglar Mouse began to drop Flawless Orbs as loot.
- 19 April 2011: The Burglar Mouse dropped the Antique Scalloped Pink Egg as loot in the Bazaar as part of the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 14 April 2011: The Burglar Mouse began dropping a bonus Burglar Egg as loot as part of the annual Spring Egg Hunt.
- 25 June 2013: The Burglar Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps, with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Burglar Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 14 October 2014: The Burglar Mouse was given new art. The costumed version appeared during the Halloween 2014 event and the new uncostumed version appeared when the event ended on 4 November 2014.

The Cabin Boy Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

Hunters must have an active Pirate Bounty to attract this mouse.

Hunters must capture a Pirate Mouse to obtain a pirate bounty. Capturing all 3 mice listed on the crew manifest will reward the player with booty from the pirate bounty.

- 23 January 2012: The Calligraphy mouse was released as part of the Year of the Dragon Festival event.
- The amount of points (1,888) and gold (888) awarded on catching a Calligraphy mouse reflects the belief in the number eight being considered "lucky" in Chinese culture; a sequence of a "lucky" number is considered even more favorable!

# 🐭 Captain Crook

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

Captain Crook is a breed of mouse found on the in Cerulean Skyport.

| Captain Crook |  |  |  |

| While in pursuit of her great nemesis Ronza, this menacing marauder inadvertently discovered the Kingdom of Gnawnia, a whole new land ripe for pillage and plunder from her ravenous fleet of Sky Raiders.<br>Her armada of shiny ships full of sinister scallywags descended from the skies in great force as they tried to recover the precious chrome that they claim was stolen from them by the famous airborne merchant that Gnawnia knows so well. Thanks to the help of the MouseHunters on the land below, Ronza managed a narrow escape from the clutches of these coarse criminals but unfortunately, not before alerting them to the even greater prize of the kingdom below.<br>She docked her fleet at the Cerulean Skyport, an aerial port town that had been floating safe and undefended for countless years, and set her raiders upon its docks to take anything and everything they could get their paws on. Using these valuable, stolen commodities, the villainous Captain Crook set her sights on raiding all the helpless lands below with the ultimate goal of taking the entire kingdom as her own. She recruited many of the mice native to this new kingdom whose goals aligned with her own and together with the might of her fleet and the power of her mysterious Cerulean Chrome, she would be an unstoppable force to conquer this new continent... unless the bravest of the MouseHunters are able to stand against her. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1299 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

Captain Crook is attracted to Aurora Bocconcini but can also be encountered at a lower rate with Sky Pirate Swiss or Sky Raider Romano.

## Hunting Strategy

The Captain Crook can only be attracted while intercepting one of Captain Crook raids.

Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro, Draconic, Law power types are effective against Captain Crook.
All other types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 7 July 2026: Captain Crook was released as part of Cerulean Skyport location.

# 🐭 Caravan Guard Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Caravan Guard Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Fiery Warpath location, only where there are more than 10 mice left to defeat in Wave One, more than 18 mice left in Wave Two, or more than 26 mice left in Wave Three.

Caravan Guard mice are not known to drop any loot.

| Caravan Guard Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: The Marching Flame (Support) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 900 | Gold: | 900 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Physical, Tactical, Hydro | Other Requirements: | Wave 1,2 or 3 |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Fiery Warpath | Loot: | None |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| This mouse acts as the veins of the war - carrying the lifeblood of weapons, armor, water, and food to the battle front. An army is nothing without supplies. Failing to capture a Caravan Guard will undoubtedly strengthen the Marching Flame's forces. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 316 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Caravan Guard mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese. In fact, they are known to have an aversion to SUPER|brie+ cheese and are known to avoid traps baited with them.

## Hunting Strategy

None of the weapon types are very effective or effective against Caravan Guard mice. Physical, Hydro, and Tactical traps are less effective, and the rest are ineffective.

When a hunter fails to capture a Caravan Guard Mouse, one of the following messages will appear on the Hunter's Journal:

> Strength in numbers! After I failed to catch a Caravan Guard Mouse, members of my hunting party were able to prevent the mouse reinforcements from arriving!

Or

> Failing to capture a Caravan Guard Mouse allowed reinforcements to arrive!
More mice have been added to the wave!

When failing to catch a Caravan Guard Mouse, additional mice will be added to the current wave. If a hunter has friends currently in the Sandtail Desert region, there is a chance that additional mice will not be added. A minimum of one friend is needed in helping to prevent reinforcements from arriving. However, no matter how many friends a hunter has in this region, it is not a 100% guarantee that reinforcements will be prevented.

Since failure to catch a Caravan Guard mouse carries a risk of increasing the number of mice remaining to be defeated, hunters wishing to avoid this risk are advised to use SUPER|brie+ cheese to minimize the possibility of encountering this mouse. Additionally, fewer Caravan Guard mice are attracted when any Warpath charm is armed (Warpath Archer, Warpath Soldier, Warpath Scout, Warpath Cavalry, Warpath Mage, or the super variants of these charms). In particular, arming a Warpath Mage Charm or Warpath Cavalry Charm in Wave 1 can be an effective way to adjust the population without having any charms consumed.

## History and Trivia

- Caravan Guard Mice were introduced on 22 March 2011 with the release of the Sandtail Desert region.

# 🐭 Carmine the Apothecary

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Carmine the Apothecary is a breed of mouse that can be found in the Living Garden.

After capturing the Carmine the Apothecary in the Living Garden, the hunter is transferred to a different version of the location, the Twisted Garden. This also will allow travel to the alternate version of the Lost City, the Cursed City, and the alternate version of the Sand Dunes, the Sand Crypts. If the Hunter hunts with an incorrect cheese at any time, the Hunter will be returned to the normal versions of these locations.

| Carmine the Apothecary |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Living Garden Mice |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 12,500 | Gold: | 800 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Hydro | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Duskshade Camembert | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Living Garden | Loot: | None |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Tales are still told of the sickness that ran through Muridae: First, mice would break out in feverish sweats, then they'd develop spots all over their body, and then finally collapse comatose. This illness spread like wildfire and it seemed nothing could be done, until finally Carmine arrived with faintly-glowing poultices and potions and miraculously cured everyone in a single day.<br>Since then, she's been the town's famed and well-loved apothecary, able to cure diseases that don't even have names yet. Sneaking into the Living Garden has allowed her to gather the Garden Essences necessary to accomplish this task, but something sinister happened to her there, and she's become more than just an apothecary - for reasons unknown she's raised an army of mice that attack anyone entering the Garden, and is able to wield incredible powers and seemingly span worlds with her magic!<br>The question is, how did a simple apothecary gain the ability to control world-rending magic, and what does this mean for Gnawnia as a whole? |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 410 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Carmine the Apothecary is only attracted to Duskshade Camembert.

## Hunting Strategy

The Carmine the Apothecary is a member of the Living Garden mouse group. As such all power types except Hydro weapons are ineffective against it.

## History and Trivia

- 14 November 2012: The Carmine the Apothecary was released as a part of the Living Garden update.
- The in-game description for the Carmine the Apothecary changes after the Hunter first catches her.

| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Physical | Other Requirements: | Dungeon Floor |

| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Some mice work very hard and eventually find a career that is dynamic, engaging, and fulfilling. This is not one of those mice. The Cell Sweeper has been tasked with the thankless job of cleaning prison cells in an infinite dungeon. It's certainly not how he envisioned his future but it's not all bad, sometimes he can faintly hear the sounds of music, laughter, and enjoyment bleeding down from the ballroom above. At least, that's what he assumes those sounds are, since he's never actually experienced any of those things firsthand. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1130 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

Cell Sweeper Mouse is only attracted to Beanster Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

The Cell Sweeper Mouse can only be attracted after planting a Short Vine to the Dungeon Floor.

Physical power type is effective against Cell Sweeper Mouse.
All other types are ineffective.

## Loot

Cell Sweeper Mouse can drop:

- Lavish Lapis Bean

- 9 May 2023: Cell Sweeper Mouse was released as part of Bountiful Beanstalk location.

# 🐭 Centaur Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

| Centaur Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Forest Guild |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 3,800 | Gold: | 960 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Tactical | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Standard CheeseGnarled CheeseWicked Gnarly Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Great Gnarled TreeLagoon | Loot: | Desert Travel AlmanacGnarled Potion |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| These noble mice are most often found traveling through Whisker Woods to and from Gnawnia. Known for their curiosity, Centaur mice collect cheese and other items from the various Kingdoms they visit. Although peaceful by nature, Centaur mice will not hesitate to defend their fellow mice, for they see MouseHunters as a nuisance that must be defeated. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 32 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

In the Great Gnarled Tree location, the Centaur Mouse will be attracted when hunting with Standard Cheese and Gnarled Cheese.
In the Lagoon location, the Centaur Mouse will be attracted when hunting with Gnarled Cheese and Wicked Gnarly Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Centaur Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tactical | PhysicalShadowArcaneHydroLaw | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Centaur Mouse in the Great Gnarled Tree and Lagoon locations.

## Loot

The Centaur Mouse drops Gnarled Potions as loot in the Great Gnarled Tree location.

The Centaur Mouse will drop the Desert Travel Almanac in the Great Gnarled Tree or Lagoon locations when working the Zurreal Trap Research Assignment.
The Desert Travel Almanac is necessary for crafting the Zurreal's Folly weapon.

The Centaur Mouse is not known to drop any other loot in the Lagoon location.

## History and Trivia

- 9 April 2009: The Centaur Mouse is released for the first time in the Great Gnarled Tree.
- 22 April 2009: The Centaur Mouse begins appearing in the Lagoon.
- 14 April 2011: The Centaur Mouse began dropping a bonus Whisker Woods Egg as loot for the first time, as part of the annual Spring Egg Hunt.
- 23 August 2011: The Centaur Mouse began to drop the Desert Travel Almanac as loot.
- 25 June 2013: The Centaur Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law traps with the release of Varmint Valley.

Centaur Ranger mice have been found to be especially attracted to String Cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

The Centaur Ranger Mouse is weak to Rift Traps. It can only be attracted by String Cheese.

The Centaur Ranger Mouse is only available when rage in the Gigantic Gnarled Tree section in Whisker Woods Rift is at 50.

- The Centaur Ranger Mouse drops the Enlarged Rift Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Centaur Ranger mice were introduced on 28 January 2015, with the introduction of the Whisker Woods Rift.
- Centaur Ranger mice are the Rift version of Centaur mice.

| Atop the tall tower, in the midst of an isle of madness, sits the maddest mouse of them all: the Chess Master. The Seasonal Garden and the Tower answer to his whims, changing with his mercurial moods. Behind his glowing white eyes lies the mind which produced the rage of the Technic King, and the cold brutality of the Mystic Queen. The Chess Master has the potential to create anything- but he is more likely to destroy everything. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 278 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Chess Master is only attracted to Checkmate Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

The Chess Master can be attracted using Checkmate Cheese once the Hunter catches either a Mystic King or a Technic King and stays in the tower (by either using Checkmate Cheese or Stalemate Charm).

Tactical traps are very effective against the Chess Master, while all others are ineffective.

Catching the Chess Master will cause the Hunter to be moved to the Seasonal Garden unless Stalemate Charm is also armed. If the Hunter manages to stay in Zugzwang's Tower after catching the Chess Master (by using Stalemate Charm), they can attempt to catch more mice, including the Chess Master itself.

Catching a Chess Master with a Uncharged Scholar Charm armed will add a Scholar Charm to the Hunter's inventory. This charm can be used to attract Zurreal the Eternal in Crystal Library.

## History and Trivia

- 26 August 2010: The Chess Master was released as part of the Zugzwang's Tower.
- The Chess Master is pictured wearing one sock on the right foot; its mate is Zugzwang's Left Sock dropped as loot.
- 29 August 2012: The Chess Master began dropping Ancient Relic as loot.

| Chipper mice aren't as chipper as you might expect, given their names. Instead, they all seem to have a chip on their shoulder, and are bitter, spiteful fellows. Chippers work out their frustrations on the ice and rocks in front of them, making quick work of walls and carving tunnels faster than would seem otherwise possible.<br>In fact, the only other activity that seems to give them solace is stealing hunters' supplies and hiding their ill-gotten goods in holes made slightly too small for human hands. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 371 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Chipper Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

In the Slushy Shoreline, the Chipper Mouse is known to drop War Scrap as loot.
The Chipper Mouse can be found in the Treacherous Tunnels, Bombing Run, and The Mad Depths zones of Iceberg. The encounter rate for the Chipper Mouse can be increased by arming the Iceberg Boiler Base, Magnet Base, or Ultimate Iceberg Base.

## History and Trivia

- 7 May 2012:: The Chipper Mouse was released in Slushy Shoreline. It was found to drop War Supplies as loot until 14 May 2012.
- Between 14 May 2012 and 24 May 2012: The Chipper Mouse dropped Interlocking Hull Fragments.
- 1 June 2012: The Chipper Mouse began to drop loot in the Iceberg.

Chitinous mice have been found to be especially attracted to Pungent Havarti cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

- Shadow type traps such as the Clockapult of Time are very effective against Chitinous mice. All traps with other power types are ineffective against these mice.

## History and Trivia

- Chitinous mice began dropping the Antique Scalloped Pink Egg as loot, on 19 April 2011, for the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- During the Spring Egg Hunt 2011, Chitinous mice also dropped a bonus Jungle of Dread Egg as loot for hunters who had obtained the Rocket Rabbit Mallet. They dropped the same loot the following year during the Spring Egg Hunt 2012 event for hunters who had obtained the Eggscavator.
- 29 July 2015: The Chitinous Mouse stopped dropping Laboratory Map Piece.

## History and Trivia

- 3 December 2009: The Christmas Tree Mouse was released for the first time as part of the Great Winter Hunt '09 event and dropped Icing Sugar as loot.
- 12 December 2009: The Christmas Tree Mouse began to drop both Gingerbread Base Blueprints and Gingerbread House Plans in the Festive Comet.
- 14 December 2009: The Christmas Tree Mouse began to drop Three Santa Hats in the Festive Comet.
- 22 December 2009: The Christmas Tree Mouse began to drop Eleven Coloured Lights in the Festive Comet.
- 23 December 2009: The Christmas Tree Mouse began to drop Twelve Liters of Egg Nog in the Festive Comet.
- 2 January 2010: The Christmas Tree Mouse was released into the Harbour for a period of time to make up for a glitch and give players a chance at another set of Gingerbread Base Blueprints or Gingerbread House Plans. The glitch prevented players from collecting a second set of Gingerbread Base Blueprints or Gingerbread House Plans after failing to craft the item. It also dropped Frozen Fromage.
- 13 December 2016: The points/gold for Christmas Tree Mouse were changed.

The Corkataur Mouse is only attracted to Wildfire Queso.

## Hunting Strategy

Only Draconic traps are effective against the Corkataur Mouse, all other power types are ineffective. The Corkataur Mouse can only be encountered in Queso Geyser while gathering cork bark.

Dragonbane Charm, Super Dragonbane Charm, Extreme Dragonbane Charm, and Ultimate Dragonbane Charm provide a power bonus against the Corkataur Mouse.

## History and Trivia

- 30 April 2019: The Corkataur Mouse was introduced, with the release of the Queso Geyser area.

# 🐭 Corrupt Commodore Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Corrupt Commodore Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Harbour.

| Corrupt Commodore Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Dock Dwellers |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 400 | Gold: | 600 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Hydro | Other Requirements: | Pirate Bounty |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Harbour | Loot: | Flawed OrbSatchel of Gold (250 gold)Swiss Cheese |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| A defector from the Royal Mouse Navy, these mice still wear the fancy outfits and come with advanced nautical training but have switched sides in search of glory and treasure. To be their own mouse and to answer to no one is truly the dream! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 861 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

The Corrupt Commodore Mouse has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Hunters must have an active Pirate Bounty to attract this mouse.

Hunters must capture a Pirate Mouse to obtain a pirate bounty. When Hunters begin the search for the mice listed on the crew manifest provided by the Pirate Mouse, the 5 Pirate Crew mice are added to the mouse population of the Harbour. Capturing all 3 mice listed on the crew manifest will reward the player with booty from the pirate bounty.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Corrupt Commodore Mouse drops the Pirate Crew Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- 11 April 2017: The Corrupt Commodore Mouse was added to the game with the introduction of new content to the Harbour location.
- 17 October 2018: The Corrupt Commodore Mouse changed mouse groups from Aquatic Order to Dock Dwellers.

# 🐭 Corrupt Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Corrupt Mouse is a breed of mouse that can be found in the Cursed City. It is not known to drop any loot.

| Corrupt Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Lost City Mice |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 8,250 | Gold: | 1,250 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Arcane | Other Requirements: | See Fear, Darkness, and Mist Curses Minigame |
| Cheese: | Graveblossom Camembert | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Cursed City | Loot: | None |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Long before the curse descended on the Cursed City, this mouse was known for his disreputable rituals. Since then, however, he has gained in power immensely, and aids in perpetuating those curses.<br>Hunters can actively work to dispel these curses and pursue the Essence Guardian Mouse, but the Corrupt Mouse will periodically check in to renew them, so you'll have to remain vigilant! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 411 |  |  |

## Hunting Strategy

The Corrupt Mouse is only attracted to Graveblossom Camembert.

It is a member of the Lost City Mice group. As such all power types except Arcane are ineffective against it.

During the Fear, Darkness, and Mist Curses Minigame, the Corrupt Mouse cannot be attracted in the first three hunts in Cursed City after all three curses have been lifted. If the Hunter is using a Shattering Charm, the Corrupt Mouse cannot be encountered in the first five hunts instead.

## History and Trivia

- 14 November 2012: The Corrupt Mouse was introduced with the release of the Cursed City location.
- Prior to 14 January 2013, it was possible to have three hunts without attracting a Corrupt Mouse after any one curse was lifted, rather than after all three curses.

| The Year of the Dragon brings many great wonders, but this Lunar New Year's Dragon Dance is a little different. The dragon costume itself has been enchanted by the mice at Zugzwang's Tower for a most magical display, however, it seems things have gotten a little bit out of control. Information on how to locate and capture the Costumed Dragon Mouse can be found on the current Lunar New Year location page.

## History and Trivia

- 23 January 2012: The Costumed Dragon Mouse was released as part of the Year of the Dragon Festival event.
- The amount of points (2,888) and gold (888) awarded on catching a Costumed Dragon Mouse reflects the belief of the number eight being considered "lucky" in Chinese culture; a sequence of a "lucky" number is considered even more favorable!
- The description for the Costumed Dragon Mouse originally contained a sentence about charm usage:

> Use Dragonbreath Charms to attract this most ferocious beast; it's the only fireworks display that can stun them long enough to be captured!

## History and Trivia

- 11 February 2013: The Costumed Snake Mouse was introduced as part of the Year of the Snake Festival event.
- The amount of points (2,888) and gold (5,888) awarded on catching a Costumed Snake Mouse reflects the belief of the number eight being considered "lucky" in Chinese culture; a sequence of a "lucky" number is considered even more favorable!
- The description for the Costumed Snake Mouse originally included this sentence:

> Mice inside this costume become so brave that they actually become attracted to the incredible noise and lights of the otherwise-terrifying Snakebite Charm fireworks.

|  | Mouse ID#: 1189 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

Crematio Scorchworth Mouse is only attracted to Fiery Fontina.

## Hunting Strategy

Only Draconic power types are effective against Crematio Scorchworth Mouse.

## History and Trivia

- 6 August 2024: Crematio Scorchworth Mouse was released as part of a pre-release adventure of the Draconic Depths location.
- 13 August 2024: Originally attracted by Hot Queso, Crematio Scorchworth Mouse moved to Draconic Depths as part of the official release of the location.

# 🐭 Crimson Commander Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Crimson Commander Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Fiery Warpath location during Wave One, Wave Two and Wave Three. They are not known to drop any loot.

| Crimson Commander Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: The Marching Flame (Support) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 10,000 | Gold: | 1,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | ArcaneDraconicHydroPhysicalTactical | Other Requirements: | Wave 1,2 or 3 |
| Cheese: | SUPER&#124;brie+ | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Fiery Warpath | Loot: | None |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| The Crimson Commander carries its Warmonger's orders into battle. The strategy of the entire war is entrusted to these mice. Defeating them could turn the tide of the battle. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 310 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Crimson Commander mice have been found to be especially attracted to SUPER|brie+ cheese.

## Special Effect

Upon capturing a Crimson Commander Mouse, the following message will be displayed on the Hunter's Journal:
By capturing a Crimson Commander Mouse I demoralized the remaining mice in this wave!
My current streak was x.
x of each mouse have retreated!

## Hunting Strategy

Catching a Crimson Commander Mouse, particularly during a streak of unbroken catches of a specific Marching Flame class or unit (Archer, Scout, Warrior, Cavalry, Mage or Artillery), will help to reduce the remaining quantities of mice that require defeating in that current Wave.
Hunters have the option of arming Warpath Commander's Charms or Super Warpath Commander's Charms to help increase the likelihood of encountering a Crimson Commander Mouse.

During Wave One, Crimson Commander Mice retreat when there are less than 11 mice remaining in the troops to be defeated.
During Wave Two, Crimson Commander Mice retreat when there are less than 19 mice remaining in the troops to be defeated.
During Wave Three, Crimson Commander Mice retreat when there are less than 27 mice remaining in the troops to be defeated.

## Event Participation

- The Crimson Commander Mouse drops the Warpath Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Crimson Commander Mice were introduced on 22 March 2011 with the release of the Sandtail Desert region.
- During the Spring Egg Hunt 2011, Crimson Commander Mice began dropping a bonus Warpath Egg as loot for hunters who had obtained the Rocket Rabbit Mallet. They dropped the same loot the following year during the Spring Egg Hunt 2012 event for hunters who had obtained the Eggscavator.

# 🐭 Crown Collector Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

| Crown Collector Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Indigenous Mice (Rare Rodent) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 1,000 | Gold: | 1,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | All Power Types except Parental | Other Requirements: | Attraction Rate is affected by thenumber of silver and gold Crownsa Hunter has obtained. |
| Cheese: | SUPER&#124;brie+ | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | BazaarCrystal LibraryKing's ArmsS.S. Huntington IV | Loot: | None |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Crown Collector mice were renowned for their rarity and difficulty to catch. Even such powerful mice as the Dojo Sensei came to them for advice on how to properly pillage pesky Hunters!<br>Over time, these mice began to charge a fee for their advice... and their only fee was the crowns that the King awarded to his most elite Hunters. Now obsessed with gaining more and more crowns, these mice most often visit the traps of hunters who have earned several crowns themselves. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 350 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

The Crown Collector Mouse has a strong attraction for SUPER|brie+ but can also be attracted using Standard Cheese.

In the Bazaar location, the Crown Collector Mouse can also be attracted when hunting with Gilded Cheese.
Aboard the S.S. Huntington IV, the Crown Collector Mouse can also be attracted when hunting with Galleon Gouda.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Crown Collector Mouse.

The Attraction Rate of the Crown Collector Mouse is affected by the number of silver and gold Crowns a Hunter has obtained. As the number of collected crowns increases, the encounter rate for this mouse will improve, though the effect will be small.

## Power Type Effectiveness

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | All Power Types except Parental | None |

## History and Trivia

- 5 August 2011: The Crown Collector Mouse was painted live during an 'All about the art' Feedback Friday. Ideas and suggestions for how the mouse should look were provided by the audience at the time of painting.
- 12 August 2011: The Crown Collector Mouse was introduced in recognition of the MouseHunt Century Club.
- 25 June 2013: The Crown Collector Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law power type traps with the release of Varmint Valley.
- 25 March 2014: The Crown Collector Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 06 May 2020: The Crown Collector Mouse was removed from Tournament Hall and added to the King's Arms.

| Mouse Group: Followers of Furoma |  |  |  |

| Love is in the air, and so is this mouse! Firing their heart-tipped arrows, these mice are responsible for an occasional slight increase in the mouse population. Hunters who get caught in the crossfire may have second thoughts about using their deadly traps on the little mice. Hey, even Zombie Mice deserve some love!<br>In his quest to perfect the art of spreading love, he spends his time honing his archery skills. With each arrow he aims, he seeks to not only unite hearts but to also hit the mark with precision and grace. Through rigorous archery training, he ensures that each and every shot has the potential to ignite the flames of love and romance, bringing mice (and sometimes hunters) together! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 44 |  |  |

## History and Trivia

- 10 February 2009: The Cupid Mouse was released as part of the Valentine's Giveaway event.
- Prior to 2012, the Cupid Mouse was found to be especially attracted to SUPER|brie+ cheese.
- 10 February 2014: Prior to the Valentine's population shift, the Cupid Mouse could only be found in the Iceberg and Acolyte Realm phases of Calamity Carl's Cozy Cruise.
- The description of the Cupid Mouse has been shortened since its release. The original description was:

> Love is in the air, and so is this mouse! Firing their heart-tipped arrows, these mice are responsible for a slight increase in the mouse population during February. Hunters who get caught in the crossfire may have second thoughts about using their deadly traps on the little mice. Hey, even zombie mice deserve some love! This mouse is available around Valentine's Day.

- 19 March 2024: The Cupid Mouse was relocated to the Training Grounds at the beginning of the Spring Egg Hunt 2024 event. Prior to this relocation, the Cupid Mouse was available in the Valentine's Day or Lunar New Year events.

| Sky Pirate Seal | Always Drops The amount a mouse drops depends on the mouse caught. |
| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

Additionally, the Cutthroat Cannoneer Mouse can drop the following loots as well:

- Bottled Wind
- Cyclone Stone

## History and Trivia

- 08 September 2020: The Cutthroat Cannoneer Mouse was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

# 🐭 Cutthroat Pirate Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Cutthroat Pirate Mouse is a breed of mouse found on the Floating Islands, on any island with a Sky Pirate Den present.

| Cutthroat Pirate Mouse |  |  |  |

| Mouse Group: Floating Islanders (Sky Pirate) |  |  |  |

Their aerial combat skills are so formidable that they have earned the admiration of the local parrot population who have willingly chosen to join their dastardly cause. |  |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

The Cutthroat Pirate Mouse is only attracted to Sky Pirate Swiss Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

All powertypes except Rift and Parental are effective against the Cutthroat Pirate Mouse. The Cutthroat Pirate Mouse can only be encountered on an island with one or multiple Sky Pirate Den present and active.

Cutthroat Pirate Mouse has a chance to drop Storm Cell if Rocket Boosters have been attached on the Hunter's Dirigible and the following loot depending on the buffs present:

| Sky Ore | Always drops Boosted by Sky Ore Deposit and Loot Caches |

| Cloud Curd | Drops when a Cloud Curd Bonus is present Can be boosted by Loot Caches |

Additionally, the Cutthroat Pirate Mouse can drop the following loots as well:

- 08 September 2020: The Cutthroat Pirate Mouse was introduced with the release of the Floating Islands.

|  | Mouse ID#: 692 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Cyclops Barbarian mice have been found to be especially attracted to String Cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

## Hunting Strategy

The Cyclops Barbarian Mouse is weak to Rift Traps. It can only be attracted by String Cheese.

The Cyclops Barbarian Mouse is only available when rage in the Crazed Clearing section in Whisker Woods Rift is at 50.

Catching a Cyclops Barbarian Mouse will reset the rage levels of all three factions in the Whisker Woods Rift unless a Taunting Charm is armed.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Cyclops Barbarian Mouse drops the Enlarged Rift Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Cyclops Barbarian mice were introduced on 28 January 2015, with the introduction of the Whisker Woods Rift.
- Cyclops Barbarian mice are the Rift version of Cyclops mice.

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

| Cyclops Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Forest Guild |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 2,800 | Gold: | 2,800 |

| Required Power Types: | Tactical | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | Standard CheeseGnarled CheeseWicked Gnarly Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Calm ClearingLagoon | Loot: | Cherry PotionGreat Gnarled Tree Map PieceSimple Orb |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Cyclops mice are known for their immense size and strength, which makes up for their lack of intelligence. Hunters are often confused by the behaviour of a Cyclops mouse, unsure if their tendency to run right at a trap is due to stupidity or confidence in their own strength. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 46 |  |  |

## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

In the Calm Clearing location, the Cyclops Mouse will be attracted when hunting with Standard Cheese and Cherry Cheese.
In the Lagoon location, the Cyclops Mouse will only be attracted when hunting with Gnarled Cheese and Wicked Gnarly Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Cyclops Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tactical | PhysicalShadowArcaneHydroLaw | None |

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Cyclops Mouse in the Calm Clearing and Lagoon locations.

Hunters must capture the Cyclops Mouse in the Calm Clearing location in order to obtain the Great Gnarled Tree Map Piece which is required to enter the Great Gnarled Tree location.

## Loot

The Cyclops Mouse drops the following loot:

- Calm Clearing

> Cherry Potion
Great Gnarled Tree Map Piece
Simple Orb

- Lagoon

> Simple Orb

## History and Trivia

- 31 March 2009: The Cyclops Mouse was released with the introduction of the Calm Clearing.
- 25 January 2011: The Cyclops Mouse began to drop Simple Orb as loot.
- 25 June 2013: The Cyclops Mouse gained a vulnerability to Law traps with the release of Varmint Valley
- 29 July 2015: The strength of the Cyclops Mouse was reduced and it's chance of encounter was increased.
- 29 July 2015: The Gnarled Tree Map Piece was renamed the Great Gnarled Tree Map Piece.
